		command = { trigger = { exists = CGX } type = relation which = CGX value = 15 }
		command = { trigger = { exists = CSX } type = relation which = CSX value = 20 }

		command = { type = secedeprovince which = CHI value = 2551 when = 2 }
		command = { type = capital which = 2551 } # Chongqing +
	}
}

}

# United front

	id = 2012015

event = {
	id = 2012025
	random = no
	country = U59

	trigger = {
		exists = CHI
		random = 35
		NOT = { war = { country = CHI country = U59 } }
		NOT = { war = { country = CHI country = JAP } }
		NOT = { alliance = { country = USA country = CGX } }
		NOT = { alliance = { country = JAP country = CGX } }
		NOT = { alliance = { country = ENG country = CGX } }
		NOT = { alliance = { country = SOV country = CGX } }
		OR = { #either CGX is not a puppet, or it is a puppet of CHI
			NOT = { ispuppet = CGX }
			puppet = { country = CGX country = CHI }
		}
		OR = { #either U59 is not a puppet, or it is a puppet of CHI
			NOT = { ispuppet = U59 }
			puppet = { country = U59 country = CHI }
		}
	}

	name = EVT_27008_NAME
	desc = EVT_27008_DESC
	style = 2
	picture = "news_paper"

	date = { day = 0 month = may year = 1936 }

		ai_chance = 100
		command = { type = sleepminister which = 1146403 }
		
		command = { type = dissent value = 5 } #15-20 dissent

		command = { type = end_puppet }
		command = { type = event which = 2400530 where = U59 when = 0 } #independent state
		#command = { type = switch_allegiance which = -1 where = CHI }
		#command = { type = war value = CHC }
		command = { type = event which = 2012026 where = CGX when = 0 }
		command = { type = event which = 2012026 where = CSX when = 0 }

		NOT = { war = { country = CHI country = JAP } }
		NOT = { war = { country = CHC country = JAP } }
		OR = {
			year = 1947
			event = { id = 2011032 days = 360 } #A year since japanese surrender
			event = { id = 2011028 days = 360 } #A year since japanese surrender
			war = { country = CHI country = CHC }
		}
		random = 50
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1938 }
	offset = 35
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_2053000_ACTA
		command = { type = leave_alliance when = 1 }
		command = { type = dissent 		value = -5 } # the time is ripe for the Revolution
		command = { type = event which = 2053001 where = CHI when = 0 }
		
		#command = { type = capital which = 1379 }
		
		command = { trigger = { ai = yes } type = local_clrflag which = garSpec }
		command = { trigger = { ai = yes } type = event which = 2400012 where = CHC when = 0 } #gar20
		#Best to leave it on. CHC does some really dumb invasions, but turning it off would mean that CHI will have no reason to even garrison the beaches or Taiwan at all
		#command = { type = local_clrflag which = InvShutOff }
		#command = { trigger = { ai = yes } type = event which = 2400032 where = CHC when = 0 } #invasionOff
		command = { trigger = { ai = CHI } type = event which = 2400012 where = CHI when = 0 } #gar20
		command = { trigger = { ai = CHI } type = event which = 2400186 where = CHI when = 0 } #redeploy30
		
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { local_flag = war_preparedness } } type = local_setflag which = war_preparedness when = 0 value = 5  } #No war preparedness debuff	

		command = { type = local_setflag 	which = inMajorWar	 }

event = {
    id = 1330110180
    random = no
    country = CHI
	save_date = yes

	name = EVT_1330110180_NAME
	desc = EVT_1330110180_DESC
	style = 2
	
	decision = {
		alliance = { country = CHC }
		event = { id = 2011000 days = 360 } #from marco polo
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		NOT = { dissent = 10 }
		NOT = { flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 50 } }  #Japan is not steamrolling China
		OR = { 
			NOT = { flag = OperationZ } #USSR already cancelled aid
			event = 550 #US started lend lease, no longer need Soviet aid
			war = { country = JAP country = USA }
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			AND = {
				event = 540 #US export control act
				flag = { which = CHC_aggressive_count when = 1 value = 18 } #over 3 years of communist provocations (~early 1941)
				event = { id = 1330110170 days = 150 } #5 months since 100 regiments offensive (august -> january)
			}
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		alliance = { country = CHC }
		event = { id = 2011000 days = 360 } #from marco polo
		NOT = { dissent = 10 }
		NOT = { flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 50 } }  #Japan is not steamrolling China
		OR = { 
			NOT = { flag = OperationZ } #USSR already cancelled aid
			event = 550 #US started lend lease, no longer need Soviet aid
			war = { country = JAP country = USA }
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			AND = {
				event = 540 #US export control act
				flag = { which = CHC_aggressive_count when = 1 value = 18 } #over 3 years of communist provocations (~early 1941)
				event = { id = 1330110170 days = 150 } #5 months since 100 regiments offensive (august -> january)
			}
		}
	}
	
	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1956 }
	
	action = {
        name = EVT_1330110180_ACTA
		command = { type = dissent value = 3 }
		command = { type = manpowerpool value = -100  } #Unpopular move, so less potential future recruites

		command = { type = event which = 1330110181 where = JAP when = 0 }
		#command = { type = access which = CHC }
	}
